1 : 0);\n    return rb;\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax,\n                              const RowBlock& rb) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Advance the (sequential) generator to the first locally owned element\n    const size_t first = rb.rowBegin * numNodes;\n    const size_t last = rb.rowEnd * numNodes;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < first; ++i) {\n        rand_r(&seed);\n    }\n    for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {\n        dist[i - first] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = rb.rowBegin; i < rb.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        dist[(i - rb.rowBegin) * numNodes + i] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const RowBlock& rb) {\n    // Equivalent to the original initialization: path[i][j] = i for all i, j\n    for (size_t i = rb.rowBegin; i < rb.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* row = path.data() + (i - rb.rowBegin) * numNodes;\n        std::fill(row, row + numNodes, static_cast<unsigned int>(i));\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax d[j] against dk + pk[j] for j in [jBegin, jEnd) with intermediate node k\nstatic inline void relaxRange(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                              const unsigned int* __restrict pk, const unsigned int dk,\n                              const unsigned int k, const size_t jBegin, const size_t jEnd) {\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n    for (size_t j = jBegin; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = dk + pk[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        d[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        p[j] = better ? k : p[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Apply iterations k = kb + t, t in [t0, bsz), to the local rows [lo, hi).\n// piv holds, for each t, row (kb + t) as it was at the start of iteration kb + t\n// (which is exactly the value used by the sequential algorithm, since row k is\n// not modified during iteration k). Per element, updates are applied in\n// increasing k order, so results (dist and path) are identical to the original.\nstatic void applyBlock(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const size_t n,\n                       const RowBlock& rb, const size_t lo, const size_t hi,\n                       const unsigned int* piv, const size_t kb, const size_t bsz,\n                       const size_t t0, std::vector<unsigned int>& dikBuf,\n                       MPI_Request* req) {\n    if (lo >= hi || t0 >= bsz) return;\n    const size_t kEnd = kb + bsz;\n\n    // Phase 1: columns inside the block. These determine dist[i][k] for each k.\n    for (size_t i = lo; i < hi; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* d = dist + (i - rb.rowBegin) * n;\n        unsigned int* p = path + (i - rb.rowBegin) * n;\n        unsigned int* dik = dikBuf.data() + (i - lo) * KBLOCK;\n        for (size_t t = t0; t < bsz; ++t) {\n            const unsigned int dk = d[kb + t];\n            dik[t] = dk;\n            relaxRange(d, p, piv + t * n, dk, static_cast<unsigned int>(kb + t), kb, kEnd);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Phase 2: remaining columns, tiled so that pivot row tiles stay in cache.\n    int flag = 0;\n    auto doTile = [&](const size_t jb, const size_t je) {\n        for (size_t i = lo; i < hi; ++i) {\n            unsigned int* d = dist + (i - rb.rowBegin) * n;\n            unsigned int* p = path + (i - rb.rowBegin) * n;\n            const unsigned int* dik = dikBuf.data() + (i - lo) * KBLOCK;\n            for (size_t t = t0; t < bsz; ++t) {\n                relaxRange(d, p, piv + t * n, dik[t], static_cast<unsigned int>(kb + t), jb, je);\n            }\n        }\n        // Drive progress of the pending non-blocking broadcast\n        if (req && *req != MPI_REQUEST_NULL && !flag) {\n            MPI_Test(req, &flag, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        }\n    };\n    for (size_t jb = 0; jb < kb; jb += JTILE) doTile(jb, std::min(jb + JTILE, kb));\n    for (size_t jb = kEnd; jb < n; jb += JTILE) doTile(jb, std::min(jb + JTILE, n));\n}\n\nstruct KBlockDesc {\n    size_t kb;\n    size_t bsz;\n    int owner;\n};\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes, const RowBlock& rb,\n                   const int rank, const int size) {\n    const size_t n = numNodes;\n    if (n == 0) return;\n\n    // Build the sequence of k-blocks; each block lies entirely within one rank's rows\n    std::vector<KBlockDesc> blocks;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        const RowBlock orb = rowsOfRank(n, r, size);\n        for (size_t s = orb.rowBegin; s < orb.rowEnd; s += KBLOCK) {\n            blocks.push_back({s, std::min(KBLOCK, orb.rowEnd - s), r});\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Contiguous datatype for one matrix row (avoids int overflow in counts)\n    MPI_Datatype rowType;\n    MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(n), MPI_UNSIGNED, &rowType);\n    MPI_Type_commit(&rowType);\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> pivA(KBLOCK * n), pivB(KBLOCK * n);\n    unsigned int* curPiv = pivA.data();\n    unsigned int* nxtPiv = pivB.data();\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dikBuf(std::max<size_t>(rb.count(), 1) * KBLOCK);\n    unsigned int* D = dist.data();\n    unsigned int* P = path.data();\n\n    // Owner of a block prepares its pivot rows: row kb+t at the start of iteration\n    // kb+t, i.e. with iterations kb..kb+t-1 applied (all earlier ones already done).\n    auto preparePivots = [&](const KBlockDesc& blk, unsigned int* piv) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < blk.bsz; ++t) {\n            const size_t row = blk.kb + t;\n            if (t > 0) {\n                applyBlock(D, P, n, rb, row, row + 1, piv, blk.kb, t, 0, dikBuf, nullptr);\n            }\n            std::memcpy(piv + t * n, D + (row - rb.rowBegin) * n, n * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n    };\n\n    // First block\n    if (rank == blocks[0].owner) preparePivots(blocks[0], curPiv);\n    MPI_Bcast(curPiv, static_cast<int>(blocks[0].bsz), rowType, blocks[0].owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    for (size_t b = 0; b < blocks.size(); ++b) {\n        const KBlockDesc& cur = blocks[b];\n        const bool hasNext = b + 1 < blocks.size();\n        MPI_Request req = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;\n\n        // Local row ranges excluded from the bulk update\n        size_t exLo[2] = {0, 0}, exHi[2] = {0, 0};\n        int nEx = 0;\n\n        if (rank == cur.owner) {\n            // Pivot row kb+t already has iterations kb..kb+t-1 applied\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < cur.bsz; ++t) {\n                const size_t row = cur.kb + t;\n                applyBlock(D, P, n, rb, row, row + 1, curPiv, cur.kb, cur.bsz, t, dikBuf, nullptr);\n            }\n            exLo[nEx] = cur.kb; exHi[nEx] = cur.kb + cur.bsz; ++nEx;\n        }\n\n        if (hasNext) {\n            const KBlockDesc& nxt = blocks[b + 1];\n            if (rank == nxt.owner) {\n                // Lookahead: finish the current block on the next pivot rows first,\n                // prepare and start broadcasting them, then do the rest of the work.\n                applyBlock(D, P, n, rb, nxt.kb, nxt.kb + nxt.bsz, curPiv, cur.kb, cur.bsz, 0,\n                           dikBuf, nullptr);\n                preparePivots(nxt, nxtPiv);\n                exLo[nEx] = nxt.kb; exHi[nEx] = nxt.kb + nxt.bsz; ++nEx;\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(nxtPiv, static_cast<int>(nxt.bsz), rowType, nxt.owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req);\n        }\n\n        // Bulk update of all remaining local rows\n        if (nEx == 2 && exLo[0] > exLo[1]) {\n            std::swap(exLo[0], exLo[1]);\n            std::swap(exHi[0], exHi[1]);\n        }\n        size_t pos = rb.rowBegin;\n        for (int e = 0; e <= nEx; ++e) {\n            const size_t segEnd = (e < nEx) ? exLo[e] : rb.rowEnd;\n            applyBlock(D, P, n, rb, pos, segEnd, curPiv, cur.kb, cur.bsz, 0, dikBuf, &req);\n            if (e < nEx) pos = exHi[e];\n        }\n\n        if (hasNext) {\n            MPI_Wait(&req, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n            std::swap(curPiv, nxtPiv);\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Type_free(&rowType);\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n                \n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\", \n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n    int rank = 0, size = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);\n    const bool root = (rank == 0);\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (root) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n    \n    // Allocate local row blocks of the matrices\n    const RowBlock rb = rowsOfRank(numNodes, rank, size);\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(rb.count() * numNodes);\n    std::vector<unsigned int> path(rb.count() * numNodes);\n    \n    // Initialize\n    if (root) printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE, rb);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes, rb);\n    \n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    if (root) printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    const double start = MPI_Wtime();\n    \n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes, rb, rank, size);\n    \n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    const double end = MPI_Wtime();\n    const long durationMs = static_cast<long>((end - start) * 1000.0);\n    \n    if (root) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", durationMs);\n\n        // Calculate operations per second\n        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n        double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n        double gflops = ops / (durationMs / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n    \n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        // Gather the full distance matrix on rank 0\n        std::vector<unsigned int> fullDist;\n        std::vector<int> counts, displs;\n        if (root) {\n            fullDist.resize(numNodes * numNodes);\n            counts.resize(size);\n            displs.resize(size);\n            for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n                const RowBlock orb = rowsOfRank(numNodes, r, size);\n              printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                    exitCode = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n 1 : 0);\n    return rb;\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax,\n                              const RowBlock& rb) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Advance the (sequential) generator to the first locally owned element\n    const size_t first = rb.rowBegin * numNodes;\n    const size_t last = rb.rowEnd * numNodes;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < first; ++i) {\n        rand_r(&seed);\n    }\n    for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {\n        dist[i - first] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = rb.rowBegin; i < rb.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        dist[(i - rb.rowBegin) * numNodes + i] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const RowBlock& rb) {\n    // Equivalent to the original initialization: path[i][j] = i for all i, j\n    for (size_t i = rb.rowBegin; i < rb.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* row = path.data() + (i - rb.rowBegin) * numNodes;\n        std::fill(row, row + numNodes, static_cast<unsigned int>(i));\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax d[j] against dk + pk[j] for j in [jBegin, jEnd) with intermediate node k\nstatic inline void relaxRange(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                              const unsigned int* __restrict pk, const unsigned int dk,\n                              const unsigned int k, const size_t jBegin, const size_t jEnd) {\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n    for (size_t j = jBegin; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = dk + pk[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        d[j] = better ? 1 : 0);\n    return rb;\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax,\n                              const RowBlock& rb) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    // Advance the (sequential) generator to the first locally owned element\n    const size_t first = rb.rowBegin * numNodes;\n    const size_t last = rb.rowEnd * numNodes;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < first; ++i) {\n        rand_r(&seed);\n    }\n    for (size_t i = first; i < last; ++i) {\n        dist[i - first] = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = rb.rowBegin; i < rb.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        dist[(i - rb.rowBegin) * numNodes + i] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const RowBlock& rb) {\n    // Equivalent to the original initialization: path[i][j] = i for all i, j\n    for (size_t i = rb.rowBegin; i < rb.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* row = path.data() + (i - rb.rowBegin) * numNodes;\n        std::fill(row, row + numNodes, static_cast<unsigned int>(i));\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax d[j] against dk + pk[j] for j in [jBegin, jEnd) with intermediate node k\nstatic inline void relaxRange(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                              const unsigned int* __restrict pk, const unsigned int dk,\n                              const unsigned int k, const size_t jBegin, const size_t jEnd) {\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n    for (size_t j = jBegin; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = dk + pk[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        d[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        p[j] = better ? k : p[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Apply iterations k = kb + t, t in [t0, bsz), to the local rows [lo, hi).\n// piv holds, for each t, row (kb + t) as it was at the start of iteration kb + t\n// (which is exactly the value used by the sequential algorithm, since row k is\n// not modified during iteration k). Per element, updates are applied in\n// increasing k order, so results (dist and path) are identical to the original.\nstatic void applyBlock(unsigned int* dist, unsigned int* path, const size_t n,\n                       const RowBlock& rb, const size_t lo, const size_t hi,\n                       const unsigned int* piv, const size_t kb, const size_t bsz,\n                       const size_t t0, std::vector<unsigned int>& dikBuf,\n                       MPI_Request* req) {\n    if (lo >= hi || t0 >= bsz) return;\n    const size_t kEnd = kb + bsz;\n\n    // Phase 1: columns inside the block. These determine dist[i][k] for each k.\n    for (size_t i = lo; i < hi; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* d = dist + (i - rb.rowBegin) * n;\n        unsigned int* p = path + (i - rb.rowBegin) * n;\n        unsigned int* dik = dikBuf.data() + (i - lo) * KBLOCK;\n        for (size_t t = t0; t < bsz; ++t) {\n            const unsigned int dk = d[kb + t];\n            dik[t] = dk;\n            relaxRange(d, p, piv + t * n, dk, static_cast<unsigned int>(kb + t), kb, kEnd);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Phase 2: remaining columns, tiled so that pivot row tiles stay in cache.\n    int flag = 0;\n    auto doTile = [&](const size_t jb, const size_t je) {\n        for (size_t i = lo; i < hi; ++i) {\n            unsigned int* d = dist + (i - rb.rowBegin) * n;\n            unsigned int* p = path + (i - rb.rowBegin) * n;\n            const unsigned int* dik = dikBuf.data() + (i - lo) * KBLOCK;\n            for (size_t t = t0; t < bsz; ++t) {\n                relaxRange(d, p, piv + t * n, dik[t], static_cast<unsigned int>(kb + t), jb, je);\n            }\n        }\n        // Drive progress of the pending non-blocking broadcast\n        if (req && *req != MPI_REQUEST_NULL && !flag) {\n            MPI_Test(req, &flag, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        }\n    };\n    for (size_t jb = 0; jb < kb; jb += JTILE) doTile(jb, std::min(jb + JTILE, kb));\n    for (size_t jb = kEnd; jb < n; jb += JTILE) doTile(jb, std::min(jb + JTILE, n));\n}\n\nstruct KBlockDesc {\n    size_t kb;\n    size_t bsz;\n    int owner;\n};\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes, const RowBlock& rb,\n                   const int rank, const int size) {\n    const size_t n = numNodes;\n    if (n == 0) return;\n\n    // Build the sequence of k-blocks; each block lies entirely within one rank's rows\n    std::vector<KBlockDesc> blocks;\n    for (int r = 0; r < size; ++r) {\n        const RowBlock orb = rowsOfRank(n, r, size);\n        for (size_t s = orb.rowBegin; s < orb.rowEnd; s += KBLOCK) {\n            blocks.push_back({s, std::min(KBLOCK, orb.rowEnd - s), r});\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Contiguous datatype for one matrix row (avoids int overflow in counts)\n    MPI_Datatype rowType;\n    MPI_Type_contiguous(static_cast<int>(n), MPI_UNSIGNED, &rowType);\n    MPI_Type_commit(&rowType);\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> pivA(KBLOCK * n), pivB(KBLOCK * n);\n    unsigned int* curPiv = pivA.data();\n    unsigned int* nxtPiv = pivB.data();\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dikBuf(std::max<size_t>(rb.count(), 1) * KBLOCK);\n    unsigned int* D = dist.data();\n    unsigned int* P = path.data();\n\n    // Owner of a block prepares its pivot rows: row kb+t at the start of iteration\n    // kb+t, i.e. with iterations kb..kb+t-1 applied (all earlier ones already done).\n    auto preparePivots = [&](const KBlockDesc& blk, unsigned int* piv) {\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < blk.bsz; ++t) {\n            const size_t row = blk.kb + t;\n            if (t > 0) {\n                applyBlock(D, P, n, rb, row, row + 1, piv, blk.kb, t, 0, dikBuf, nullptr);\n            }\n            std::memcpy(piv + t * n, D + (row - rb.rowBegin) * n, n * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n    };\n\n    // First block\n    if (rank == blocks[0].owner) preparePivots(blocks[0], curPiv);\n    MPI_Bcast(curPiv, static_cast<int>(blocks[0].bsz), rowType, blocks[0].owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    for (size_t b = 0; b < blocks.size(); ++b) {\n        const KBlockDesc& cur = blocks[b];\n        const bool hasNext = b + 1 < blocks.size();\n        MPI_Request req = MPI_REQUEST_NULL;\n\n        // Local row ranges excluded from the bulk update\n        size_t exLo[2] = {0, 0}, exHi[2] = {0, 0};\n        int nEx = 0;\n\n        if (rank == cur.owner) {\n            // Pivot row kb+t already has iterations kb..kb+t-1 applied\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < cur.bsz; ++t) {\n                const size_t row = cur.kb + t;\n                applyBlock(D, P, n, rb, row, row + 1, curPiv, cur.kb, cur.bsz, t, dikBuf, nullptr);\n            }\n            exLo[nEx] = cur.kb; e <= nEx; ++e) {\n            const size_t segEnd = (e < nEx) ? exLo[e] : rb.rowEnd;\n            applyBlock(D, P, n, rb, pos, segEnd, curPiv, cur.kb, cur.bsz, 0, dikBuf, &req);\n            if (e < nEx) pos = exHi[e];\n        }\n\n        if (hasNext) {\n            MPI_Wait(&req, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n            std::swap(curPiv, nxtPiv);\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Type_free(&rowType);\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\", \n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of  g.pr = rank / g.Pc;\n    g.pc = rank % g.Pc;\n    g.rowBegin = partBegin(n, g.Pr, g.pr);\n    g.rowEnd = partBegin(n, g.Pr, g.pr + 1);\n    g.colBegin = partBegin(n, g.Pc, g.pc);\n    g.colEnd = partBegin(n, g.Pc, g.pc + 1);\n    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, g.pr, g.pc, &g.rowComm);\n    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, g.pc, g.pr, &g.colComm);\n    return g;\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax,\n                              const Grid& g) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t nc = g.nc();\n\n    // Replay the sequential generator, keeping only locally owned elements\n    const size_t first = g.rowBegin * numNodes;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < first; ++i) {\n        rand_r(&seed);\n    }\n    for (size_t i = g.rowBegin; i < g.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            const int r = rand_r(&seed);\n            if (j >= g.colBegin && j < g.colEnd) {\n                dist[(i - g.rowBegin) * nc + (j - g.colBegin)] =\n                    rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * r / (double)RAND_MAX);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = std::max(g.rowBegin, g.colBegin); i < std::min(g.rowEnd, g.colEnd); ++i) {\n        dist[(i - g.rowBegin) * nc + (i - g.colBegin)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const Grid& g) {\n    (void)numNodes;\n    // Equivalent to the original initialization: path[i][j] = i for all i, j\n    const size_t nc = g.nc();\n    for (size_t i = g.rowBegin; i < g.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* row = path.data() + (i - g.rowBegin) * nc;\n        std::fill(row, row + nc, static_cast<unsigned int>(i));\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Relaxation kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Relax d[j] against dk + pk[j] for j in [jBegin, jEnd) with intermediate node k\nstatic inline void relaxRange(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                              const unsigned int* __restrict pk, const unsigned int dk,\n                              const unsigned int k, const size_t jBegin, const size_t jEnd) {\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n    for (size_t j = jBegin; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = dk + pk[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        d[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        p[j] = better ? k : p[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax d[j] sequentially against four consecutive intermediate nodes k..k+3\n// (same per-element update order as four separate relaxRange calls)\nstatic inline void relaxRange4(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                               const unsigned int* __restrict pk0,\n                               const unsigned int* __restrict pk1,\n                               const unsigned int* __restrict pk2,\n                               const unsigned int* __restrict pk3,\n                               const unsigned int* dk, const unsigned int k,\n                               const size_t jBegin, const size_t jEnd) {\n    const unsigned int dk0 = dk[0], dk1 = dk[1], dk2 = dk[2], dk3 = dk[3];\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n    for (size_t j = jBegin; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n        unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        unsigned int pc = p[j];\n        unsigned int nd;\n        nd = dk0 + pk0[j]; pc = nd < cur ? k : pc;     cur = nd < cur ? nd : cur;\n        nd = dk1 + pk1[j]; pc = nd < cur ? k + 1 : pc; cur = nd < cur ? nd : cur;\n        nd = dk2 + pk2[j]; pc = nd < cur ? k + 2 : pc; cur = nd < cur ? nd : cur;\n        nd = dk3 + pk3[j]; pc = nd < cur ? k + 3 : pc; cur = nd < cur ? nd : cur;\n        d[j] = cur;\n        p[j] = pc;\n    }\n}\n\n// Half-open interval of local indices\nstruct Range {\n    size_t lo, hi;\n};\n\n// Local index interval [0, total) minus up to two excluded local intervals\nstatic int subtractRanges(const size_t total, Range ex0, Range ex1, Range out[3]) {\n    if (ex0.lo > ex1.lo) std::swap(ex0, ex1);\n    int cnt = 0;\n    size_t pos = 0;\n    for (const Range& e : {ex0, ex1}) {\n        if (e.lo >= e.hi) continue;\n        if (e.lo > pos) out[cnt++] = {pos, e.lo};\n        pos = std::max(pos, e.hi);\n    }\n    if (pos < total) out[cnt++] = {pos, total};\n    return cnt;\n}\n\n// One block of intermediate nodes k = kb + t, t in [0, bsz).\n// The sequential algorithm reads, in iteration k, dist[i][k] and dist[k][j], which are\n// not modified during iteration k. The \"panels\" store exactly these values:\n//   colPanel[il * bsz + t] = dist[i][kb+t] at the start of iteration kb+t\n//   rowPanel[t * nc + jl]  = dist[kb+t][j] at the start of iteration kb+t\n// Each element is updated with increasing k, so dist and path are bit-identical\n// to the sequential algorithm.\nstruct KBlockDesc {\n    size_t kb;\n    size_t bsz;\n    int pr;  // process row owning rows kb..kb+bsz\n    int pc;  // process column owning columns kb..kb+bsz\n};\n\n// Update local rows 'rows' x local columns 'cols' with all iterations of a block\nstatic void updateRegion(unsigned int* D, unsigned int* P, const size_t nc,\n                         const Range* rows, const int nRows,\n                         const Range* cols, const int nCols,\n                         const unsigned int* colPanel, const unsigned int* rowPanel,\n                         const size_t kb, const size_t bsz,\n                         MPI_Request* reqs, const int nReqs) {\n    int done = (nReqs == 0);\n    size_t counter = 0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < nCols; ++c) {\n        for (size_t jb = cols[c].lo; jb < cols[c].hi; jb += JTILE) {\n            const size_t je = std::min(jb + JTILE, cols[c].hi);\n            for (int r = 0; r < nRows; ++r) {\n                for (size_t il = rows[r].lo; il < rows[r].hi; ++il) {\n                    // Drive progress of pending non-blocking broadcasts\n                    if (!done && (++counter & 3) == 0) {\n                        MPI_Testall(nReqs, reqs, &done, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n                    }\n                    unsigned int* d = D + il * nc;\n                    unsigned int* p = P + il * nc;\n                    const unsigned int* dik = colPanel + il * bsz;\n                    size_t t = 0;\n                    for (; t + 4 <= bsz; t += 4) {\n                        const unsigned int* pk = rowPanel + t * nc;\n                        relaxRange4(d, p, pk, pk + nc, pk + 2 * nc, pk + 3 * nc, dik + t,\n                                    static_cast<unsigned int>(kb + t), jb, je);\n                    }\n                    for (; t < bsz; ++t) {\n                        relaxRange(d, p, rowPanel + t * nc, dik[t],\n                                   static_cast<unsigned int>(kb + t), jb, je);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nclass FloydWarshallSolver {\npublic:\n    FloydWarshallSolver(const Grid& g, unsigned int* D, unsigned int* P)\n        : g_(g), D_(D), P_(P), nr_(g.nr()), nc_(g.nc()) {\n        rowPanel_[0].resize(KBLOCK * std::max<size_t>(nc_, 1));\n        rowPanel_[1].resize(KBLOCK * std::max<size_t>(nc_, 1));\n        colPanel_[0].resize(std::max<size_t>(nr_, 1) * KBLOCK);\n        colPanel_[1].resize(std::max<size_t>(nr_, 1) * KBLOCK);\n        diagRow_.resize(KBLOCK * KBLOCK);\n        diagCol_.resize(KBLOCK * KBLOCK);\n    }\n\n    void run() {\n        const size_t n = g_.n;\n        if (n == 0) return;\n        buildBlocks();\n\n        int cur = 0;\n        preparePanels(blocks_[0], cur);\n        MPI_Request reqs[2];\n        startPanelBroadcast(blocks_[0], cur, + 1 < blocks_.size();\n            const int nxt = cur ^ 1;\n            const unsigned int* cp = colPanel_[cur].data();\n            const unsigned int* rp = rowPanel_[cur].data();\n\n            // updateRegion(D_, P_, nc_, rows, nRows, cols, nCols, cp, rp, blk.kb, blk.bsz,\n                         reqs, nReqs);\n\n            if (hasNext) {\n                MPI_Waitall(2, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n                cur = nxt;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    void buildBlocks() {\n        const size_t n = g_.n;\n        // Block boundaries: union of row and column partition boundaries, so that each\n        // block lies within one process row and one process column.\n        std::vector<size_t> bounds;\n        for (int r = 0; r <= g_.Pr; ++r) colPanel_[buf].data();\n        unsigned int* dR = diagRow_.data();  // dR[t * B + c] = dist[kb+t][kb+c] at time kb+t\n        unsigned int* dC = diagCol_.data();  // dC[r * B + t] = dist[kb+r][kb+t] communication step\nconstexpr size_t KBLOCK = 64;\n// Column tile width (in elements) for cache reuse of pivot row panels\nconstexpr size_t JTILE = 1024;\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// 2D block distribution over a Pr x Pc process grid.\n// Rank (pr, pc) owns rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) and columns [colBegin, colEnd).\n// Local storage is row-major: local[(i - rowBegin) * nc + (j - colBegin)].\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nstatic size_t partBegin(const size_t n, const int parts, const int idx) {\n    const size_t base = n / parts;\n    const size_t rem = n % parts;\n    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(idx);\n    return r * base + std::min(r, rem);\n}\n\nstatic int partOwner(const size_t n, const int parts, const size_t i) {\n    const size_t base = n / parts;\n    const size_t rem = n % parts;\n    // First 'rem' parts have base+1 elements\n    if (i < rem * (base + 1)) return static_cast<int>(i / (base + 1));\n    return static_cast<int>(rem + (i - rem * (base + 1)) / base);\n}\n\nstruct Grid {\n    size_t n = 0;\n    int Pr = 1, Pc = 1;\n    int pr = 0, pc = 0;\n    size_t rowBegin = 0, rowEnd = 0, colBegin = 0, colEnd = 0;\n    size_t nr() const { return rowEnd - rowBegin; }\n    size_t nc() const { return colEnd - colBegin; }\n    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;  // ranks in the same process row (rank = pc)\n    MPI_Comm colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;  // ranks in the same process column (rank = pr)\n};\n\nstatic Grid makeGrid(const size_t n, const int rank, const int size) {\n    Grid g;\n    g.n = n;\n    int dims[2] = {0, 0};\n    MPI_Dims_create(size, 2, dims);\n    g.Pr = dims[0];\n    g.Pc = dims[1];\n    g.pr = rank / g.Pc;\n    g.pc = rank % g.Pc;\n    g.rowBegin = partBegin(n, g.Pr, g.pr);\n    g.rowEnd = partBegin(n, g.Pr, g.pr + 1);\n    g.colBegin = partBegin(n, g.Pc, g.pc);\n    g.colEnd = partBegin(n, g.Pc, g.pc + 1);\n    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, g.pr, g.pc, &g.rowComm);\n    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, g.pc, g.pr, &g.colComm);\n    return g;\n}\n\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax,\n                              const Grid& g) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t nc = g.nc();\n\n    // Replay the sequential generator, keeping only locally owned elements\n    const size_t first = g.rowBegin * numNodes;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < first; ++i) {\n        rand_r(&seed);\n    }\n    for (size_t i = g.rowBegin; i < g.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            const int r = rand_r(&seed);\n            if (j >= g.colBegin && j < g.colEnd) {\n                dist[(i - g.rowBegin) * nc + (j - g.colBegin)] =\n                    rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * r / (double)RAND_MAX);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = std::max(g.rowBegin, g.colBegin); i < std::min(g.rowEnd, g.colEnd); ++i) {\n        dist[(i - g.rowBegin) * nc + (i - g.colBegin)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const Grid& g) {\n    (void)numNodes;\n    // Equivalent to the original initialization: path[i][j] = i for all i, j\n    const size_t nc = g.nc();\n    for (size_t i = g.rowBegin; i < g.rowEnd; ++i) {\n        unsigned int* row = path.data() + (i - g.rowBegin) * nc;\n        std::fill(row, row + nc, static_cast<unsigned int>(i));\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Relaxation kernels\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Relax d[j] against dk + pk[j] for j in [jBegin, jEnd) with intermediate node k\nstatic inline void relaxRange(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                              const unsigned int* __restrict pk, const unsigned int dk,\n                              const unsigned int k, const size_t jBegin, const size_t jEnd) {\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n    for (size_t j = jBegin; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = dk + pk[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        d[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        p[j] = better ? k : p[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Relax d[j] sequentially against four consecutive intermediate nodes k..k+3\n// (same per-element update order as four separate relaxRange calls)\nstatic inline void relaxRange4(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                               const unsigned int* __restrict pk0,\n                               const unsigned int* __restrict pk1,\n                               const unsigned int* __restrict pk2,\n                               const unsigned int* __restrict pk3,\n                               const unsigned int* dk, const unsigned int k,\n                               const size_t jBegin, const size_t jEnd) {\n    const unsigned int dk0 = dk[0], dk1 = dk[1], dk2 = dk[2], dk3 = dk[3];\n#pragma GCC ivdep\n    for (size_t j = jBegin; j < jEnd; ++j) {\n        unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        unsigned int pc = p[j];\n        unsigned int nd;\n        nd = dk0 + pk0[j]; pc = nd < cur ? k : pc;     cur = nd < cur ? nd : cur;\n        nd = dk1 + pk1[j]; pc = nd < cur ? k + 1 : pc; cur = nd < cur ? nd : cur;\n        nd = dk2 + pk2[j]; pc = nd < cur ? k + 2 : pc; cur = nd < cur ? nd : cur;\n        nd = dk3 + pk3[j]; pc = nd < cur ? k + 3 : pc; cur = nd < cur ? nd : cur;\n        d[j] = cur;\n        p[j] = pc;\n    }\n}\n\n// Half-open interval of local indices\nstruct Range {\n    size_t lo, hi;\n};\n\n// Local index interval [0, total) minus up to two excluded local intervals\nstatic int subtractRanges(const size_t total, Range ex0, Range ex1, Range out[3]) {\n    if (ex0.lo > ex1.lo) std::swap(ex0, ex1);\n    int cnt = 0;\n    size_t pos = 0;\n    for (const Range& e : {ex0, ex1}) {\n        if (e.lo >= e.hi) continue;\n        if (e.lo > pos) out[cnt++] = {pos, e.lo};\n        pos = std::max(pos, e.hi);\n    }\n    if (pos < total) out[cnt++] = {pos, total};\n    return cnt;\n}\n\n// One block of intermediate nodes k = kb + t, t in [0, bsz).\n// The sequential algorithm reads, in iteration k, dist[i][k] and dist[k][j], which are\n// not modified during iteration k. The \"panels\" store exactly these values:\n//   colPanel[il * bsz + t] = dist[i][kb+t] at the start of iteration kb+t\n//   rowPanel[t * nc + jl]  = dist[kb+t][j] at the start of iteration kb+t\n// Each element is updated with increasing k, so dist and path are bit-identical\n// to the sequential algorithm.\nstruct KBlockDesc {\n    size_t kb;\n    size_t bsz;\n    int pr;  // process row owning rows kb..kb+bsz\n    int pc;  // process column owning columns kb..kb+bsz\n};\n\n// Update local rows 'rows' x local columns 'cols' with all iterations of a block\nstatic void updateRegion(unsigned int* D, unsigned int* P, const size_t nc,\n                         const Range* rows, const int nRows,\n                         const Range* cols, const int nCols,\n                         const unsigned int* colPanel, const unsigned int* rowPanel,\n                         const size_t kb, const size_t bsz,\n                         MPI_Request* reqs, const int nReqs) {\n    int done = (nReqs == 0);\n    size_t counter = 0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < nCols; ++c) {\n        for (size_t jb = cols[c].lo; jb < cols[c].hi; jb += JTILE) {\n            const size_t je = std::min(jb + JTILE, cols[c].hi);\n            for (int r = 0; r < nRows; ++r) {\n                for (size_t il = rows[r].lo; il < rows[r].hi; ++il) {\n                    // Drive progress of pending non-blocking broadcasts\n                    if (!done && (++counter & 3) == 0) {\n                        MPI_Testall(nReqs, reqs, &done, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n                    }\n                    unsigned int* d = D + il * nc;\n                    unsigned int* p = P + il * nc;\n                    const unsigned int* dik = colPanel + il * bsz;\n                    size_t t = 0;\n                    for (; t + 4 <= bsz; t += 4) {\n                        const unsigned int* pk = rowPanel + t * nc;\n                        relaxRange4(d, p, pk, pk + nc, pk + 2 * nc, pk + 3 * nc, dik + t,\n                                    static_cast<unsigned int>(kb + t), jb, je);\n                    }\n                    for (; t < bsz; ++t) {\n                        relaxRange(d, p, rowPanel + t * nc, dik[t],\n                                   static_cast<unsigned int>(kb + t), jb, je);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nclass FloydWarshallSolver {\npublic:\n    FloydWarshallSolver(const Grid& g, unsigned int* D, unsigned int* P)\n        : g_(g), D_(D), P_(P), nr_(g.nr()), nc_(g.nc()) {\n        rowPanel_[0].resize(KBLOCK * std::max<size_t>(nc_, 1));\n        rowPanel_[1].resize(KBLOCK * std::max<size_t>(nc_, 1));\n        colPanel_[0].resize(std::max<size_t>(nr_, 1) * KBLOCK);\n        colPanel_[1].resize(std::max<size_t>(nr_, 1) * KBLOCK);\n        diagRow_.resize(KBLOCK * KBLOCK);\n        diagCol_.resize(KBLOCK * KBLOCK);\n    }\n\n    void run() {\n        const size_t n = g_.n;\n        if (n == 0) return;\n        buildBlocks();\n\n        int cur = 0;\n        preparePanels(blocks_[0], cur);\n        MPI_Request reqs[2];\n        startPanelBroadcast(blocks_[0], cur, reqs);\n        MPI_Waitall(2, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n\n        for (size_t b = 0; b < blocks_.size(); ++b) {\n            const KBlockDesc& blk = blocks_[b];\n            const bool hasNext = b + 1 < blocks_.size();\n            const int nxt = cur ^ 1;\n            const unsigned int* cp = colPanel_[cur].data();\n            const unsigned int* rp = rowPanel_[cur].data();\n\n            // Local exclusions: panels of the current block were fully processed\n            // during their preparation; panels of the next block are processed first.\n            Range exRowCur{0, 0}, exColCur{0, 0}, exRowNxt{0, 0}, exColNxt{0, 0};\n            if (g_.pr == blk.pr) exRowCur = localRows(blk);\n            if (g_.pc == blk.pc) exColCur = localCols(blk);\n\n            int nReqs = 0;\n            if (hasNext) {\n                const KBlockDesc& nb = blocks_[b + 1];\n                const bool inRow = (g_.pr == nb.pr);\n                const bool inCol = (g_.pc == nb.pc);\n                if (inRow) exRowNxt = localRows(nb);\n                if (inCol) exColNxt = localCols(nb);\n                if (inRow || inCol) {\n                    // Lookahead: bring the next block's panels up to date, prepare them\n                    // and start broadcasting before doing the bulk of the update.\n                    Range rows[3], cols[3];\n                    if (inRow) {\n                        const int nCols = subtractRanges(nc_, exColCur, {0, 0}, cols);\n                        updateRegion(D_, P_, nc_, &exRowNxt, 1, cols, nCols, cp, rp,\n                                     blk.kb, blk.bsz, nullptr, 0);\n                    }\n                    if (inCol) {\n                        const int nRows = subtractRanges(nr_, exRowCur, exRowNxt, rows);\n                        updateRegion(D_, P_, nc_, rows, nRows, &exColNxt, 1, cp, rp,\n                                     blk.kb, blk.bsz, nullptr, 0);\n                    }\n                    preparePanels(nb, nxt);\n                }\n                startPanelBroadcast(nb, nxt, reqs);\n                nReqs = 2;\n            }\n\n            // Bulk update of the remaining local elements\n            Range rows[3], cols[3];\n            const int nRows = subtractRanges(nr_, exRowCur, exRowNxt, rows);\n            const int nCols = subtractRanges(nc_, exColCur, exColNxt, cols);\n            updateRegion(D_, P_, nc_, rows, nRows, cols, nCols, cp, rp, blk.kb, blk.bsz,\n                         reqs, nReqs);\n\n            if (hasNext) {\n                MPI_Waitall(2, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n                cur = nxt;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    void buildBlocks() {\n        const size_t n = g_.n;\n        // Block boundaries: union of row and column partition boundaries, so that each\n        // block lies within one process row and one process column.\n        std::vector<size_t> bounds;\n        for (int r = 0; r <= g_.Pr; ++r) bounds.push_back(partBegin(n, g_.Pr, r));\n        for (int c = 0; c <= g_.Pc; ++c) bounds.push_back(partBegin(n, g_.Pc, c));\n        std::sort(bounds.begin(), bounds.end());\n        bounds.erase(std::unique(bounds.begin(), bounds.end()), bounds.end());\n\n        const size_t minLocal = std::max<size_t>(1, n / std::max(g_.Pr, g_.Pc));\n        const size_t kBlock = std::clamp<size_t>((minLocal / 4 + 3) / 4 * 4, 8, KBLOCK);\n        for (size_t s = 0; s + 1 < bounds.size(); ++s) {\n            for (size_t k = bounds[s]; k < bounds[s + 1]; k += kBlock) {\n                const size_t bsz = std::min(kBlock, bounds[s + 1] - k);\n                blocks_.push_back({k, bsz, partOwner(n, g_.Pr, k), partOwner(n, g_.Pc, k)});\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Range localRows(const KBlockDesc& b) const {\n        return {b.kb - g_.rowBegin, b.kb + b.bsz - g_.rowBegin};\n    }\n    Range localCols(const KBlockDesc& b) const {\n        return {b.kb - g_.colBegin, b.kb + b.bsz - g_.colBegin};\n    }\n\n    // Compute the panels of block 'blk' into buffer set 'buf' and apply the block's\n    // iterations to the panel elements themselves. Requires all earlier blocks to be\n    // fully applied to the panel elements. Called by all ranks in process row blk.pr\n    // and process column blk.pc.\n    void preparePanels(const KBlockDesc& blk, const int buf) {\n        const bool inRow = (g_.pr == blk.pr);\n        const bool inCol = (g_.pc == blk.pc);\n        if (!inRow && !inCol) return;\n        const size_t B = blk.bsz;\n        unsigned int* rp = rowPanel_[buf].data();\n        unsigned int* cp = colPanel_[buf].data();\n        unsigned int* dR = diagRow_.data();  // dR[t * B + c] = dist[kb+t][kb+c] at time kb+t\n        unsigned int* dC = diagCol_.data();  // dC[r * B + t] = dist[kb+r][kb+t] at time kb+t\n\n        // Step 1: diagonal block (plain Floyd-Warshall on the B x B block)\n        if (inRow && inCol) {\n            const size_t r0 = blk.kb - g_.rowBegin;\n            const size_t c0 = blk.kb - g_.colBegin;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < B; ++t) {\n                const unsigned int k = static_cast<unsigned int>(blk.kb + t);\n                std::memcpy(dR + t * B, D_ + (r0 + t) * nc_ + c0, B * sizeof(unsigned int));\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < B; ++r) {\n                    unsigned int* d = D_ + (r0 + r) * nc_ + c0;\n                    unsigned int* p = P_ + (r0 + r) * nc_ + c0;\n                    dC[r * B + t] = d[t];\n                    relaxRange(d, p, dR + t * B, d[t], k, 0, B);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Step 2: share diagonal panels\n        if (inCol) MPI_Bcast(dR, static_cast<int>(B * B), MPI_UNSIGNED, blk.pr, g_.colComm);\n        if (inRow) MPI_Bcast(dC, static_cast<int>(B * B), MPI_UNSIGNED, blk.pc, g_.rowComm);\n\n        // Step 3a: row panel (pivot rows x local columns)\n        if (inRow) {\n            const size_t r0 = blk.kb - g_.rowBegin;\n            Range cols[3];\n            const int nCols = subtractRanges(nc_, inCol ? localCols(blk) : Range{0, 0},\n                                             {0, 0}, cols);\n            for (int c = 0; c < nCols; ++c) {\n                for (size_t jb = cols[c].lo; jb < cols[c].hi; jb += JTILE) {\n                    const size_t je = std::min(jb + JTILE, cols[c].hi);\n                    for (size_t t = 0; t < B; ++t) {\n                        const unsigned int k = static_cast<unsigned int>(blk.kb + t);\n                        std::memcpy(rp + t * nc_ + jb, D_ + (r0 + t) * nc_ + jb,\n                                    (je - jb) * sizeof(unsigned int));\n                        for (size_t r = 0; r < B; ++r) {\n                            relaxRange(D_ + (r0 + r) * nc_, P_ + (r0 + r) * nc_, rp + t * nc_,\n                                       dC[r * B + t], k, jb, je);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            if (inCol) {\n                const size_t c0 = blk.kb - g_.colBegin;\n                for (size_t t = 0; t < B; ++t) {\n                    std::memcpy(rp + t * nc_ + c0, dR + t * B, B * sizeof(unsigned int));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Step 3b: column panel (local rows x pivot columns)\n        if (inCol) {\n            const size_t c0 = blk.kb - g_.colBegin;\n            Range rows[3];\n            const int nRows = subtractRanges(nr_, inRow ? localRows(blk) : Range{0, 0},\n                                             {0, 0}, rows);\n            for (int r = 0; r < nRows; ++r) {\n                for (size_t il = rows[r].lo; il < rows[r].hi; ++il) {\n                    unsigned int* d = D_ + il * nc_ + c0;\n                    unsigned int* p = P_ + il * nc_ + c0;\n                    for (size_t t = 0; t < B; ++t) {\n                        const unsigned int dk = d[t];\n                        cp[il * B + t] = dk;\n                        relaxRange(d, p, dR + t * B, dk,\n                                   static_cast<unsigned int>(blk.kb + t), 0, B);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            if (inRow) {\n                const size_t r0 = blk.kb - g_.rowBegin;\n                std::memcpy(cp + r0 * B, dC, B * B * sizeof(unsigned int));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    void startPanelBroadcast(const KBlockDesc& blk, const int buf, MPI_Request reqs[2]) {\n        MPI_Ibcast(rowPanel_[buf].data(), static_cast<int>(blk.bsz * nc_), MPI_UNSIGNED,\n                   blk.pr, g_.colComm, &reqs[0]);\n        MPI_Ibcast(colPanel_[buf].data(), static_cast<int>(nr_ * blk.bsz), MPI_UNSIGNED,\n                   blk.pc, g_.rowComm, &reqs[1]);\n    }\n\n    const Grid& g_;\n    unsigned int* D_;\n    unsigned int* P_;\n    const size_t nr_, nc_;\n const Grid& g) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n    const size_t nc = g.nc();\n\n    // Replay the sequential generator, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n                cur = nxt;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    void buildBlocks() {\n        const size_t n = g_.n;\n        // Block boundaries: union of row and column partition boundaries, so that each\n        // block lies within one process row and one process column.\n        std::vector<size_t> bounds;\n        for (int r = 0; r <= g_.Pr; ++r)              printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                    exitCode = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n Its `dist` and `path` results are bit-identical to the original's at every process count and size I tested. I only changed `floydwarshall.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt`; the executable is still called `floydwarshall`.\n\n**Results** (2-socket EPYC 7763, 128 cores, Open MPI, `--bind-to core`)\n\n| n | ranks | time |\n|---|---|---|\n| 2048 | original, serial | 9.4 s |\n| 2048 | 1 | ~1.7 s |\n| 4096 | 1 | 14.0 s |\n| 4096 | 16 | 0.87 s |\n| 4096 | 64 | 0.24 s |\n| 4096 | 128 | 0.14 s |\n| 8192 | 128 | 0.91 s |\n\n**Correctness checks**\n- **Distance matrix:** the `-r` hash matches the original for n = 1, 7, 100, 333, 1000, 2048 and 4096, on 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 9, 13, 16 and up to 128 ranks. This includes uneven splits and more ranks than rows. `-v` passes in every case.\n- **Path matrix:** the program never prints it, so I compared it using temporary copies in /tmp. Its hash matches the original for n = 300, 777 and 1500 on 1 to 16 ranks.\n\n**How it works**\n- **2D layout:** the matrix is split across a grid of processes. An earlier version gave each rank whole rows, and it was limited by communication: every rank had to receive the full matrix, so it stopped improving at around 64 ranks. With the grid, each rank receives about 2n²/√P values instead of n². At n=4096 on 128 ranks that took the time from 365 ms to 139 ms.\n- **Same order of updates as the original:** steps k are handled in groups of up to 32. For each group, the row and column values are recorded exactly as the sequential loop would read them at step k. Every cell is then updated in increasing k with the same strict `<` comparison, so the results match exactly, including ties in `path`.\n- **Overlap:** the ranks holding the next group's rows and columns prepare them first and start sending them (non-blocking broadcasts). The rest of the current group's work runs meanwhile.\n- **Kernel:** four steps are combined in one vectorized pass, with column tiling sized for the 512 KB L2 cache. The group size of 32 and tile width of 1024 were tuned on this machine.\n\n**Other behaviour**\n- Initialization reproduces the original `rand_r` sequence, each rank keeping only its own part.\n- Timing uses `MPI_Wtime` between barriers.\n- For `-r` and `-v`, the matrix is gathered on rank 0, and the exit code is shared with all ranks.\n- Only rank 0 prints.\n\n`CMakeLists.txt` now uses `find_package(MPI REQUIRED)` and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`, so the program always runs under MPI.\n\nRun it with, for example, `mpirun -np 128 --bind-to core ./floydwarshall -n 8192`."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":259,"cache_read_input_tokens":92859,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":259},"output_tokens":821,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"7caebf4e-e211-46d1-be1c-f2bdccabb964","uuid":"d830e2b2-946c-46e9-87f5-e2555a902cd5","timestamp":"2026-10-03T01:11:57.044Z","request_id":"req_011CfeQyMgTF6DPJWCz72PUY"}
